C11-287 · WebContent recovery and inspector lifecycle

Runtime validation PASS · all eight scenarios

Needs you: nothing. build host Validator Batch 2 at b7849c703dc5db07b0d1aad2b6125afe15b3ec68. Latest owner validation contract: ev_01M3XKZH9WH1KDK8R6NJCA81A6. This single file embeds the retained screenshots, scenario results, commands and manifests. Assembly itself ran no apps, builds or Lattice actions.

Eight numbered owner scenarios

  1. Build, launch and safety

    PASS. Exact merged-main SHA b7849c703dc5db07b0d1aad2b6125afe15b3ec68; tagged val-2 Debug built on build host. Native UI evidence was captured on the verified interactive host built-in display 1 (1800×1169), confined to the owned 1160×900 tagged window at (20,40), under bounded UI leases. build host ran build/test compute; the watched native UI used the authorized interactive host display. QA fresh/resume suppressed startup dialogs; synthesized dismissal and process absence are recorded. Five bounded UI phases completed, including the final focus rerun.

  2. Synthetic profile, browser and terminal

    PASS. Created non-default profile C11-287 QA batch2 synthetic (64A6F1BE-03FA-461B-A1A5-8EE357BFE0C8), visited example.com/?page=one then ?page=two, captured nonempty DOM snapshots and terminal marker readback. Browser and terminal painted side by side. All profile data and markers here are synthetic.

  3. Crash replacement, retained state and survival

    PASS. DEBUG termination returned scheduled=true; logs contain one replacement begin/end for the original recovery, with page=two restored. Synthetic localStorage and session cookie survived on the profile; back reached page=one and forward page=two. Terminal printed the after marker. Deferral and duplicate coalescing are executable test proof, not callback timing inferred from a socket round trip. No independent Ghostty diagnostic crash log was retained; actual terminal input and same-process UI records prove host/terminal survival for these runs.

  4. Loaded and initially empty recovery cap

    PASS. Both loaded and empty tabs recorded replacement counts 1,2,2 across three simulated terminations within ten seconds. Loaded first replacement restored the URL; empty first replacement retained new-tab state. Second replacement showed the existing error page and did not automatically reload; third was suppressed. This is the intentional error UI, not a new regression.

  5. Split resize, repaint and live snapshot

    PASS. area.resize set the split in both directions; wide-terminal, wide-browser, reset and away/back screenshots show painted areas after recovery. A subsequent live browser snapshot remained nonempty. CLI setup plus screenshots provide visual proof. Earlier pointer attempts failed the harness; no successful divider drag is claimed.

  6. Inspector recovery and teardown

    PASS. Detached recovery restored a separate inspector window with visibility true; manually closing it set visibility false and it stayed closed. Attached recovery was performed after widening the browser above 500 points: inspector remained attached and visible after replacement. Before docking was side; restored docking was bottom. This proves attached presentation class, not preservation of a precise docking edge. Native UI closed the owning browser tab with a terminal surviving, and closed the owning browser window with an attached inspector through the Close sheet while another tagged window/terminal survived. Quit with a detached inspector open and resume in browser-b completed; final cleanup reports no delayed inspector window. Cancellation before inspector attachment is proved only by the exact-head host test, not inferred from the UI runs.

  7. Merged-head executable gates

    PASS. Focused build host host invocation ddcf4bd29e7c4d108a771e4574de154e ran 31 tests and passed. It includes both inspector visibility/cancellation tests, BrowserLifecycleRegressionTests, BrowserSessionHistoryRestoreTests, CmuxWebViewLoadTrackingTests, selected portal tests, and five WebContentReplacementGateTests. Final logic invocation 397eb64ea259406f9a38c73bfe3cbd2e passed 2148 tests, three skips, zero failures using only the approved SocketControlPasswordStoreTests / WorkspaceRemoteConnectionTests class exclusions. Earlier failures remain retained; the MessagesPage test passed the same-head retry without exclusion.

  8. Focus/input baseline comparison and final dismissal

    PASS. Five baseline cycles at pre-change SHA 7bb785741750ddeb8ab12b4cf6472593fb8c3550 averaged 0.772 s; five final candidate cycles averaged 0.770 s. Same fresh 50/50 split, window geometry, fixture page, display and probe. Actual cycle values and both load averages are embedded below. Earlier candidate timings used a small divider difference and are retained separately as exploratory evidence. The timer covers PID-scoped keystroke/Return through printed-marker readback after pointer focus setup; it is not precise per-keystroke latency. No new typing stall was observed in this short probe; no soak, speedup or statistical performance conclusion is claimed. Final synthesized Quit confirmed PID absent and released the fifth bounded UI lease.

Proof limits: CLI resize with screenshots, not successful divider dragging. Attached inspector presentation retained, docking edge changed. Cancellation before attachment is host-test proof. No independent Ghostty diagnostic crash log, precise typing-latency claim, fleet soak or production release claim. No new regressions were identified in the observed scenarios.

287_basic_v1.py executable probe source
import ui_helper as h,json,time,pathlib
s=json.loads((h.ROOT/'287-setup.json').read_text());ws=s['workspace'];bid=s['browser'];tid=s['terminal'];params={'workspace_id':ws,'tab_id':bid};marker='C11_287_BATCH2_SYNTHETIC'
def bc(*args):return h.cli('browser',bid,*args)
def wait():return bc('wait','--load-state','complete','--timeout-ms','12000')
def term(mark):
 h.cli('send','--workspace',ws,'--tab',tid,"printf '"+mark+"\\n'\n")
 for _ in range(40):
  txt=h.cli('read-screen','--workspace',ws,'--tab',tid,j=False)
  if mark in txt.splitlines():return txt
  time.sleep(.05)
 raise AssertionError('No terminal output '+mark)
def records(tab=bid):
 return [x for x in pathlib.Path('/tmp/c11-debug-val-2.log').read_text().splitlines() if 'panel='+tab[:5] in x and 'browser.webview.replace.' in x]
wait();bc('storage','local','set','c11_287_probe',marker);bc('cookies','set','--name','c11_287_probe','--value',marker,'--domain','example.com','--path','/','--secure');bc('goto','https://example.com/?page=two');wait();before={'url':bc('get','url'),'storage':bc('storage','local','get','c11_287_probe'),'cookies':bc('cookies','get'),'snapshot':bc('snapshot','--interactive'),'terminal':term(marker+'_BEFORE')};assert before['snapshot']['snapshot'];h.shot('287-recovery-before');start=time.monotonic();initial=len(records());scheduled=h.rpc('debug.browser.simulate_web_content_termination',params);time.sleep(.2);wait();after={'url':bc('get','url'),'storage':bc('storage','local','get','c11_287_probe'),'cookies':bc('cookies','get'),'snapshot':bc('snapshot','--interactive'),'terminal':term(marker+'_AFTER')};assert after['url']==before['url'];assert marker in json.dumps(after['storage']) and marker in json.dumps(after['cookies']);assert after['snapshot']['snapshot'];new=records()[initial:];assert len([x for x in new if 'replace.begin' in x])==len([x for x in new if 'replace.end' in x])==1,new;h.shot('287-recovery-after');bc('back');wait();back=bc('get','url');assert '?page=one' in json.dumps(back),back;bc('forward');wait();forward=bc('get','url');assert '?page=two' in json.dumps(forward),forward
r={'before':before,'after':after,'scheduled':scheduled,'replacement_logs':new,'elapsed_s':time.monotonic()-start,'back_url':back,'forward_url':forward};(h.ROOT/'287-recovery.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2));print('PASS steps 2-3: loaded profile, retained storage/cookie, one replacement, back/forward, snapshot, terminal')
287_cap_v1.py executable probe source
import ui_helper as h,json,time,pathlib
s=json.loads((h.ROOT/'287-setup.json').read_text());ws=s['workspace'];area=s['browser_area'];all_results=[]
for kind in ('url','empty'):
 h.cli('focus-tab','--workspace',ws,'--tab',s['browser']);args=['new-tab','--workspace',ws,'--area',area,'--type','browser']
 if kind=='url':args+=['--url','https://example.com/?page=one']
 t=h.cli(*args)['tab_id'];h.cli('focus-tab','--workspace',ws,'--tab',t);params={'workspace_id':ws,'tab_id':t}
 def bc(*a):return h.cli('browser',t,*a)
 def logs():return [x for x in pathlib.Path('/tmp/c11-debug-val-2.log').read_text().splitlines() if 'panel='+t[:5] in x and 'browser.webview.replace.' in x]
 if kind=='url':
  bc('wait','--load-state','complete','--timeout-ms','10000');storage=bc('storage','local','get','c11_287_probe');assert 'C11_287_BATCH2_SYNTHETIC' in json.dumps(storage)
 before=len(logs());start=time.monotonic();events=[]
 for i in range(3):
  scheduled=h.rpc('debug.browser.simulate_web_content_termination',params);time.sleep(.18)
  if i==0 and kind=='url':bc('wait','--load-state','complete','--timeout-ms','6000')
  if i==1:bc('wait','--load-state','complete','--timeout-ms','4000')
  new=logs()[before:];count=len([x for x in new if 'replace.begin' in x]);assert count==min(i+1,2),(kind,i,count,new)
  events.append({'crash':i+1,'elapsed_s':time.monotonic()-start,'scheduled':scheduled,'replacement_count':count,'url':bc('get','url')})
  if i<2:h.shot('287-cap-'+kind+'-'+str(i+1))
 assert events[-1]['elapsed_s']<10,events
 snapshot=bc('snapshot','--interactive');assert 'Example Domain' not in snapshot.get('page',{}).get('title',''),snapshot
 r={'kind':kind,'tab':t,'events':events,'logs':logs()[before:],'error_snapshot':snapshot};all_results.append(r)
 h.cli('close-tab','--workspace',ws,'--tab',t)
(h.ROOT/'287-cap.json').write_text(json.dumps(all_results,indent=2));h.cli('focus-tab','--workspace',ws,'--tab',s['browser']);print('PASS step 4: loaded and empty tabs each replace twice, third suppressed within 10 seconds')
focus_probe.py executable probe source
import ui_helper as h,json,time,subprocess,os
s=json.loads((h.ROOT/('baseline-setup.json' if h.TAG=='val-1' else '287-setup.json')).read_text())
phase=os.environ.get('VAL_PHASE','baseline' if h.TAG=='val-1' else 'candidate')
h.activate();h.confine();results=[]
load_before=subprocess.run(['uptime'],text=True,capture_output=True).stdout.strip()
for i in range(5):
 h.click(900,300);time.sleep(.3);h.click(270,85);time.sleep(.3)
 assert h.cli('tree','--all')['active']['tab_id']==s['terminal'],'UI pointer focus did not settle onto terminal'
 marker=f'C11_287_{phase}_{i}'
 start=time.monotonic();h.type_text("printf '"+marker+"\\n'");h.enter()
 while time.monotonic()-start<5:
  text=h.cli('read-screen','--workspace',s['workspace'],'--tab',s['terminal'],j=False)
  if marker in text.splitlines():break
  time.sleep(.02)
 else:raise AssertionError('Missing printed marker '+marker)
 results.append({'cycle':i,'elapsed_s':time.monotonic()-start,'marker':marker})
h.shot('287-'+phase+'-after')
r={'tag':h.TAG,'phase':phase,'app':h.APP,'socket':h.SOCKET,'display':1,'geometry':[20,40,1160,900],'load_before':load_before,'load_after':subprocess.run(['uptime'],text=True,capture_output=True).stdout.strip(),'probe':'browser content click, terminal tab-header click, PID-scoped keystroke printf + Return, exact printed marker readback','cycles':results}
(h.ROOT/('287-'+phase+'-timings.json')).write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2));print(json.dumps(r,indent=2))
Original eight-scenario owner contract
Validator scenario now explicitly tests attached and detached inspector replacement, manual dismissal, cancellation before attachment, back/forward history and a synthetic non-default profile.

Validator scenario

1. Build merged main as a uniquely tagged Debug app on build host. Launch with C11_QA_LAUNCH=fresh using launch-tagged-automation.sh. Record merged SHA, tag, binary path, automation socket, display enumeration, and load average. Confine all UI input to the verified display and this tagged window, with a hard stop timer and proven synthesized dismissal. Expected: startup dialogs suppressed and production c11/agents untouched.
2. Create a synthetic non-default browser profile and a readable side-by-side terminal and browser area using that profile. Visit https://example.com/?page=one then https://example.com/?page=two (synthetic public fixtures), leaving back history before recovery. Capture a screenshot, current URL, and an interactive browser snapshot. Type a unique synthetic marker in the terminal and read it back. Expected: both areas paint, terminal responds, and snapshot returns a nonempty DOM tree.
3. Invoke DEBUG socket method debug.browser.simulate_web_content_termination for the browser's workspace_id/tab_id. Wait for the queued turn and page completion. Record browser.webview.replace log entries, URL, screenshot, terminal marker readback, and Ghostty log. Expected: one replacement after scheduling, same URL restored, non-default profile/data store retained, back/forward navigation still reaches both synthetic pages, terminal accepts input, no host/terminal crash. The pre-replacement return and same-turn duplicate coalescing are covered by the targeted host and gate tests; do not infer callback timing solely from a socket round trip.
4. Start a fresh browser tab on the same fixture. Simulate termination, wait for replacement, then simulate again within ten seconds. Expected: the second replacement paints the existing error page and does not automatically reload the URL. Simulate again within that window. Expected: no third makeWebView/replacement log entry, no busy recovery loop. Repeat on an initially empty tab: its first replacement remains the new-tab state, its second reaches the existing error page, and its third does not replace. Record logs/screenshots and elapsed times.
5. On a recovered browser beside the terminal, resize the split in both directions, switch away/back, and capture screenshots after each. Expected: readable areas and painted page after both split resize and crash recovery. No window-wide forced display or immediate SwiftUI layout flush is required. Run one live browser snapshot again. Expected: a nonempty tree; H-E automation fixes are not part of this PR.
6. Open Web Inspector through the tagged app's UI, and explicitly run crash replacement in both attached and detached presentations. Capture before/after screenshots while (a) closing its owning browser tab with a terminal surviving, (b) closing its owning browser window with another tagged window/terminal surviving, and (c) simulating a crash while the inspector is open. Expected: inspector closes before old host teardown; no host crash or queued reopen of a closed tab; surviving terminal input works; crash replacement reopens the inspector in its previous attached/detached presentation and keeps preferred visibility true. After restoration, manually close the detached inspector and verify it stays closed; close a tab before replacement inspector attachment and verify no delayed reopen. Also quit/relaunch the tagged app with an inspector open, proving clean dismissal and no teardown crash.
7. Inspect the exact-head build host artifacts for WebContentReplacementGateTests, the six termination tests in BrowserSessionHistoryRestoreTests, BrowserLifecycleRegressionTests, CmuxWebViewLoadTrackingTests, and the selected portal tests. Expected: behavioral assertions pass for stale/closed work, blank/nonblank cap, inspector teardown ordering, duplicate/case-varied synthetic download query resolution (first value wins), and exactly-once popup cancellation with a missing controller plus ordinary allowed navigation. These download/popup cases run through executable seams, not source greps. These selected tests already passed on the owner head; rerun on merged main if batch behavior or integration changes require it, and record the actual result.
8. Compare a short browser/terminal focus-and-input sequence against a tagged origin/main baseline from before this change, using the same fixture, geometry, display, and probe. Record both timings and both load averages; no fleet soak. Expected: surviving terminal focus/input remains responsive and no new typing stall is observed. Capture the result and dismiss the tagged app with synthesized input; verify its window/process is gone.

Shared restore smoke and harness corrections

Six-step restore smoke passed using a real local file HTML fixture. The initial missing-window report arose from a harness title filter: restored CG window title was empty while AX reported one window. Corrected PID/bounds selection captured the visible app. The Orchestrator canceled bisect; the empty title remains recorded for judgment and is not classified as a product defect. Earlier pointer resize, inspector attachment/window-close attempts and baseline focus failures are harness failures; they are not relabeled as successful UI paths. Final successful evidence above is independently identified.

Historical runtime screenshot withdrawn from public evidence under the disclosure rule. No replacement image is presented as runtime proof.

Closeout boundary

Owned tagged processes were dismissed with synthesized input and confirmed absent. Bounded UI leases were released; owned cold-read guests were removed while the foreign guest was untouched. Parent Validator owns ticket evidence posting and completion handoff. This artifact does not claim release, deployment, unfiltered full-suite success or new source changes.
